# Canonical later-synthesis intake

Revision: **ESME-CANONICAL-R1-2026-09-06**. Full population: **105/105**. This is an external lineage corpus for later Evolved-SSS synthesis, not that synthesis itself. No sibling relationships or adoption decisions are presumed. The accompanying JSON is the complete machine-readable mirror of the interfaces below.

Conditional eligibility has 83 positive values, but **no row is filtered out**. Rejected, superseded, ceremony, contested and unresolved claims constrain later reasoning; they are not controls to implement. Source lists are ancestry trails, not blanket proof. Original claim/access partitions remain attached to qualified A/B records. All rows share the global boundary that original papers were not re-accessed in this reconciliation. Failure costs and criteria retain their trigger and cheaper path; applying all mechanisms is not the composed system.

## ESME-C001 — Authorised change intent and obligation revision

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C001

**Canonical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** Change request, affected behaviour and requirements history. Requests, contracts, code, data, configuration, manuals and maintenance readiness can all be affected.

**Criterion:** An authorised change objective and explicit preserved or revised obligations can be compared with the actual result.

**Operating mechanism:** State the intended difference, affected obligations, legitimate decision maker and acceptance question before endorsing an intervention.

**Trigger:** A request, discovered defect or environmental pressure proposes changing an existing commitment.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** A short accepted issue with concrete examples is enough for a bounded low-risk change; reject or defer an unjustified request.

**Required inputs:** conditions: Decision authority and domain meaning must be established; disputed intent cannot be resolved by test count.; related_property_ids: ESME-C038; ESME-C070; interpretation: Related obligations and prerequisites, not a serial scheduling graph. Typed relations in the composition model control the relationship meaning.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** An authorised change objective and explicit preserved or revised obligations can be compared with the actual result.

**Consumer:** consumer: Those entitled to request or rely on the behaviour, not merely the test author.; decision: An authorised change objective and explicit preserved or revised obligations can be compared with the actual result.

**Authority:** actors: Those entitled to request or rely on the behaviour, not merely the test author.; boundary: Understanding or recommending a change does not itself authorise altering another actor’s obligations. Use actual agreed decision rights and capability; no new role or veto is created by this record.

**Communication/interaction:** exchange: Change request, affected behaviour and requirements history.; purpose: Enable the stated consumer to judge the mechanism, assumptions and postcondition. Use the cheapest adequate channel in NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH; mere publication is not successful communication.

**Assumptions:** INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-001; ACCOUNT: critical_assumption: Decision authority and domain meaning must be established; disputed intent cannot be resolved by test count.; discriminating_question: Which affected stakeholder is missing from the stated acceptance question?; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: B:ESME-001; ACCOUNT: needed: An identifiable baseline, affected interests and an actor entitled to resolve the objective; emergency delegation must be explicit.; failure_test: Approval can institutionalise an obsolete requirement, exclude affected users or become a bottleneck; procedural approval is not evidence that the goal is beneficial.; cheap_or_nontrigger: Use an existing unambiguous authorised request; no new board or document is needed. Decline changes without a supported objective.

**Costs/failure modes:** controlled_failure: An accepted or executed change does not correspond to a legitimate supported objective, or preservation is silently substituted for an authorised behavioural change.; known_failure_modes: INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-001; FINDINGS: Source-supported: issue/PR/test disagreement can leave correctness indeterminate. Analytical: a formal approval can still exclude affected people.; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: B:ESME-001; FINDINGS: Approval can institutionalise an obsolete requirement, exclude affected users or become a bottleneck; procedural approval is not evidence that the goal is beneficial.; This is an analytical decision model, not a proof that stakeholder agreement produces a beneficial objective. Swanson and Chapin body-access limits preclude detailed original-taxonomy claims.; coupling_costs: A mistaken objective can contaminate correct analysis.; Metric or population drift can mimic changing value.; Historical intention may remain indeterminate.; Alerts without a capable consumer accumulate noise.; The wrong test ritual can miss operative enforcement.; Deceptive suggestions can increase review burden and survive shared oracles.; Emergency labels can launder feature removal or indefinite suspension.; Local success can hide mismatched identities or permissions.; Meta-governance can consume the capacity it is meant to protect.; Compatibility layers can preserve defects indefinitely.; Duplicated approval adds ceremony if the boundaries are already jointly satisfied.; Normative ambiguity may persist after more testing.; Over-simplification can omit a genuinely multi-party decision.

## ESME-C002 — Decision-relevant, edition-aware maintenance classification

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C002

**Canonical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** Request intent and actual changed artefacts. Requests, contracts, code, data, configuration, manuals and maintenance readiness can all be affected.

**Criterion:** A mixed change can be described without hiding one purpose or treating a statistic from another taxonomy as comparable.

**Operating mechanism:** Use purpose labels alongside observable activity and artefact scope, permitting multiple purposes; include additive and emergency distinctions when using the 2022 terminology.

**Trigger:** Classification affects estimates, authorisation, reporting or selection of maintenance evidence.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Do not label every commit when the label changes no decision; one explanatory sentence may suffice.

**Required inputs:** conditions: The taxonomy edition, unit and use must be declared.; related_property_ids: ESME-C001; interpretation: Related obligations and prerequisites, not a serial scheduling graph. Typed relations in the composition model control the relationship meaning.

## ESME-C003 — Consequential non-code artefact scope

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C003

**Canonical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** Consequential code and non-code artefacts within a stated maintenance obligation.

**Criterion:** The consequential non-code objects and their consumers are in the scope, with irrelevant objects omitted explicitly when disputed.

**Operating mechanism:** Include build, configuration, data, documentation and other non-code objects only where a current or planned maintenance obligation depends on them.

**Trigger:** A maintenance task depends on consequential state outside conventional source code.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Keep the scope local; no organisation-wide inventory is required when the relevant inputs and consumers are already known.

**Required inputs:** A concrete maintenance obligation and enough impact evidence to identify consequential non-code artefacts; no general inventory requirement.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** The consequential non-code objects and their consumers are in the scope, with irrelevant objects omitted explicitly when disputed.

**Consumer:** consumer: Maintainer, operator and downstream consumer of the actual artefact.; decision: Necessary non-code changes and readiness dependencies are included in scope and assigned a consumer.

**Authority:** actors: Maintainer, operator and downstream consumer of the actual artefact.; boundary: Understanding or recommending a change does not itself authorise altering another actor’s obligations. Use actual agreed decision rights and capability; no new role or veto is created by this record.

**Communication/interaction:** exchange: Code, build inputs, schemas, configurations, operational instructions and handover material.; purpose: Enable the stated consumer to judge the mechanism, assumptions and postcondition. Use the cheapest adequate channel in NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH; mere publication is not successful communication.

**Assumptions:** INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-003; ACCOUNT: critical_assumption: Artefact relevance must be tied to a concrete maintenance obligation.; discriminating_question: What relevant non-code state is currently outside the impact account?

**Costs/failure modes:** controlled_failure: A consequential non-code dependency is omitted, leaving source changes insufficient for the actual maintenance obligation.; known_failure_modes: INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-003; FINDINGS: Source-supported: omitted non-code dependencies can break test selection; ML configuration can carry substantial system logic.; coupling_costs: A mistaken objective can contaminate correct analysis.; Hidden feedback and unenforced artefacts remain possible.

**Retirement/omission condition:** CANONICAL: Omit a mechanism when its trigger is absent or a cheaper adequate substitute establishes the actual obligation; retire it when its protected obligation or consumer ends. Do not use omission to waive an unchanged supported outcome.; INPUT_SPECIFIC: INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-003; CONDITIONS: Exclude irrelevant artefacts explicitly rather than constructing an organisation-wide inventory for a local task. Omit or retire a control when its protected obligation, relevant failure mechanism or decision consumer no longer exists; record unresolved support consequences before removing it.

## ESME-C004 — Demonstrated maintenance readiness and knowledge succession

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C004

**Canonical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** The actual recipient or continuing maintenance group and its supported work.

**Criterion:** The recipient can perform the relevant supported work, or the remaining capability/support limitation is explicit and acted upon.

**Operating mechanism:** At a transfer or continuity risk, establish that an actual willing recipient has the knowledge, access, time and practical ability to perform representative supported work; use explanation, supervised work or equivalent evidence.

**Trigger:** Initial handover, onboarding, departure, concentration or a new support obligation threatens practical maintenance capability.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** One representative diagnosis or change can suffice; stable small projects may instead make support limits or retirement explicit, without inventing extra maintainers.

**Required inputs:** A real willing recipient, relevant knowledge and artefacts, practical access, time and an opportunity to demonstrate representative supported work. Initial delivery and later turnover are different triggers of the same capability criterion.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** The recipient can perform the relevant supported work, or the remaining capability/support limitation is explicit and acted upon.

**Consumer:** Delivering and receiving maintainers exchange knowledge; managers provide time and authority rather than infer competence from attendance.

**Authority:** Delivering and receiving maintainers exchange knowledge; managers provide time and authority rather than infer competence from attendance.

**Communication/interaction:** recipient_and_boundary: Delivering and receiving maintainers exchange knowledge; managers provide time and authority rather than infer competence from attendance.; content: Use representative tasks, access checks and dialogue to test the receiving maintainer’s ability; preserve rationale and unresolved questions that the task exposes.; adequacy_test: The receiver can complete a representative task or the handoff plan changes to address demonstrated gaps.; non_requirement: No separate person, meeting, registry or tool is required merely because the analytical roles are distinct.

**Assumptions:** INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-003; ACCOUNT: critical_assumption: Artefact relevance must be tied to a concrete maintenance obligation.; discriminating_question: What relevant non-code state is currently outside the impact account?; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-064; ACCOUNT: critical_assumption: Successors have time, access, willingness and opportunities to practise; names on a roster are insufficient.; discriminating_question: What representative task demonstrates that knowledge has actually transferred?; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: B:ESME-004; ACCOUNT: needed: A receiving actor, accepted responsibility and feasible resources; possession of documents alone is insufficient.; failure_test: Guide scope establishes a planning responsibility, not a measured universal benefit of every transition artefact. Knowledge-loss proxies do not measure all expertise.; cheap_or_nontrigger: An existing competent team with unchanged access can reuse its arrangements; no separate transition ceremony is inherently required.; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: B:ESME-056; ACCOUNT: needed: A willing receiving maintainer, operational access and time to learn; a quiz alone is not enough.; failure_test: A successful rehearsal does not prove broad competence; task selection can conceal rare but important failure modes.; cheap_or_nontrigger: Pair on one meaningful change or diagnosis instead of producing a comprehensive handover manual.

**Costs/failure modes:** controlled_failure: Artefacts are handed over without a capable willing recipient able to perform supported work.; known_failure_modes: INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-003; FINDINGS: Source-supported: omitted non-code dependencies can break test selection; ML configuration can carry substantial system logic.; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-064; FINDINGS: Source-supported: truck-factor estimates have partial validation; successful-maintainer interviews are selected and do not prove one onboarding procedure.; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: B:ESME-004; FINDINGS: Guide scope establishes a planning responsibility, not a measured universal benefit of every transition artefact. ## ESME-C005 — Identifiable and recoverable maintenance baseline

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C005

**Canonical disposition:** STRONGLY_RETAINED

**Examination status:** EXAMINED

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** Source revision, build inputs, package versions, configuration and relevant data-state reference. Requests, contracts, code, data, configuration, manuals and maintenance readiness can all be affected.

**Criterion:** Another authorised maintainer can locate the baseline and reproduce the relevant comparison; restoration is separately tested.

**Operating mechanism:** Identify and retain the exact baseline and configuration needed to interpret, build, compare or restore the changed system.

**Trigger:** An intervention or investigation requires attributing differences or returning to a known state.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** A versioned file and recorded command can suffice for a local deterministic artefact; retain richer dependencies only where needed.

**Required inputs:** conditions: Referenced artefacts remain accessible and configuration identifiers resolve.; related_property_ids: ESME-C046; interpretation: Related obligations and prerequisites, not a serial scheduling graph. Typed relations in the composition model control the relationship meaning.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** Another authorised maintainer can locate the baseline and reproduce the relevant comparison; restoration is separately tested.

**Consumer:** consumer: Reviewer and operator comparing the same system, not merely matching a branch name.; decision: Another authorised maintainer can locate the baseline and reproduce the relevant comparison; restoration is separately tested.

**Authority:** actors: Reviewer and operator comparing the same system, not merely matching a branch name.; boundary: Understanding or recommending a change does not itself authorise altering another actor’s obligations. Use actual agreed decision rights and capability; no new role or veto is created by this record.

## ESME-C006 — Bounded emergency containment and deliberate degradation

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C006

**Canonical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** Live service, temporary configuration or patch and follow-up obligation. Requests, contracts, code, data, configuration, manuals and maintenance readiness can all be affected.

**Criterion:** The accepted containment objective is achieved within its loss and authority boundary, with recovery or permanent resolution still owned.

**Operating mechanism:** Authorise a bounded temporary change of objective, including disabling or degrading capability when that contains a more consequential failure; state residual obligations, accepted loss, observation and follow-up.

**Trigger:** An incident or serious exposure makes ordinary repair timing unsafe and a feasible containment action can reduce harm.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Use normal repair or deliberate non-change when urgency is absent; a simple reversible feature disablement can need only proportionate evidence.

**Required inputs:** conditions: Containment scope, operational capability and recovery or follow-up must be credible.; related_property_ids: ESME-C001; ESME-C049; ESME-C006; interpretation: Related obligations and prerequisites, not a serial scheduling graph. ## ESME-C021 — Question-selected views and reconciliation of discrepancies

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C021

**Canonical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Examination status:** EXAMINED

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** Extracted components/relations, execution observations and build mapping. Relevant source, executable traces, build descriptions, documents and human knowledge form different evidence views.

**Criterion:** The relevant conclusion states supporting views, discrepancies and remaining uncertainty.

**Operating mechanism:** Choose static, dynamic, historical and human views for the question; reconcile consequential disagreement instead of treating agreement or diagram completeness as proof.

**Trigger:** A single view leaves a consequential question unresolved or independent views disagree.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** One adequate direct view can settle a local question without a fusion framework.

## ESME-C043 — Differential comparison under a justified reference relation

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C043

**Canonical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Examination status:** EXAMINED

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** The implementations, input domain and comparison interpretation used by differential evidence.

**Criterion:** Observed differences are classified as intended, erroneous or unresolved rather than automatically counted as bugs.

**Operating mechanism:** Run comparable inputs against the old/new or independent implementations, locate differences and adjudicate them against intended obligations.

**Trigger:** A credible reference exists and executions can be made comparable.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Use direct expected results for simple cases; no duplicate implementation is required where it adds less evidence than it costs.

**Required inputs:** Comparable input/environment, known intended differences and awareness of common-mode defects.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** Observed differences are classified as intended, erroneous or unresolved rather than automatically counted as bugs.

**Consumer:** Maintainers investigate discrepancies; a reference implementation is evidence, not an unquestionable specification.

**Authority:** Maintainers investigate discrepancies; a reference implementation is evidence, not an unquestionable specification.

## ESME-C044 — Justified metamorphic relations as partial oracles

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C044

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** The justified relation between transformed inputs and expected output relations.

**Criterion:** The relation is checked for stated cases, with no claim that satisfaction proves arbitrary output correctness.

**Operating mechanism:** Choose justified input/output relations, execute related cases and investigate violations while preserving the relation’s domain assumptions.

**Trigger:** Exact expected results are difficult but a relevant mathematical or domain relation is credible.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Use ordinary assertions when expected results are cheap; do not invent weak relations to inflate a test count.

**Required inputs:** A valid metamorphic relation, appropriate numerical/nondeterministic tolerance and no unaccounted persistent side effects.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** The relation is checked for stated cases, with no claim that satisfaction proves arbitrary output correctness.

**Consumer:** Domain specialists justify relations; maintainers implement and interpret tests.

## ESME-C050 — Bounded local repair as an alternative to restructuring

## ESME-C055 — Data-conversion meaning, authority and reconciliation

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C055

**Canonical disposition:** ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** Old/new data, mapping rules, transaction history and reconciliation evidence. Baseline includes domain behaviour, live data, old/new implementations, routing and temporary synchronisation mechanisms.

**Criterion:** The converted state has justified meaning and authority within a declared scope, with divergence and unresolved losses surfaced.

**Operating mechanism:** Declare old/new data meanings, authoritative writers and reconciliation rules; check the transfer against actual data and concurrent effects instead of equating record counts or script success with preserved meaning.

**Trigger:** Schema, encoding, representation or ownership changes can alter persisted meaning.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** For stateless replacement this is not triggered; a simple verified bijective conversion may need only direct checks.

**Required inputs:** conditions: Mappings and concurrency rules must preserve or deliberately revise the required semantics.; related_property_ids: ESME-C001; ESME-C026; ESME-C049; ESME-C057; interpretation: Related obligations and prerequisites, not a serial scheduling graph. Typed relations in the composition model control the relationship meaning.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** The converted state has justified meaning and authority within a declared scope, with divergence and unresolved losses surfaced.

**Consumer:** consumer: Consumers relying on meanings such as identity, balances, ordering or provenance.; decision: Reconciled state satisfies declared invariants; unmatched or irreversible changes are explicitly resolved or accepted.

**Authority:** actors: Consumers relying on meanings such as identity, balances, ordering or provenance.; boundary: Understanding or recommending a change does not itself authorise altering another actor’s obligations. Use actual agreed decision rights and capability; no new role or veto is created by this record.

Use the cheapest adequate channel in NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH; mere publication is not successful communication.

## ESME-C058 — Coexistence cost, completion witness and scaffold retirement

**Criterion:** Removal or declared completion has evidence that the relevant old-path obligations are discharged or explicitly reassigned; remaining supported coexistence and cost are not hidden.

**Operating mechanism:** Keep temporary gateways and duplicate operation only while they protect a live obligation; before declaring completion or removing them, check residual consumers, data authority, supported versions and recovery needs, then close the old path or explicitly reclassify enduring coexistence.

## ESME-C065 — Option-preserving deferral and deliberate non-repayment

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C065

**Canonical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Examination status:** EXAMINED

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** Deferred liability, prospective demand and revisit condition. A debt item links a concrete design/implementation condition to affected future work and its economic context.

**Criterion:** Deferral is an explicit justified choice with a relevant trigger rather than forgotten work.

**Operating mechanism:** Defer a structural intervention when its expected value is below its cost, while retaining a trigger to revisit if demand, risk or support changes.

**Trigger:** Future change demand is uncertain, interest is small, or the system may retire before repayment benefits arise.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Do nothing beyond existing observation when there is no live liability; immediate repair dominates when an obligation is already breached.

## ESME-C094 — Bounded acceptance and execution authority

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C094

**Canonical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Examination status:** EXAMINED

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** SOURCE_GROUNDED_EXTRACTION

**Subject/bearer:** The acceptance decision and execution powers for a concrete intervention.

**Criterion:** The real acceptance and action stay within the delegated object, scope and capability; generated insight or test success does not enlarge them.

**Operating mechanism:** Distinguish proposing a change, accepting its evidence and executing consequential actions; bind each to actual legitimate capability and any limited delegation. Authorisation of a goal does not automatically transfer deployment or external-state powers.

**Trigger:** An actor or automated tool proposes, accepts or executes a change, especially when these functions cross authority boundaries.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** One competent authorised actor may perform all functions for a bounded change; no separate human, meeting or AI-specific approval is implied.

**Required inputs:** Legitimate authority, actual action capability and explicit bounds for any delegation.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** The real acceptance and action stay within the delegated object, scope and capability; generated insight or test success does not enlarge them.

**Consumer:** Authorised maintainers or policy holders accept changes; generators cannot enlarge their own mandate from a successful test.

**Authority:** The legitimate request/obligation owner, acceptance decision maker and action executor may differ. A goal approval or generated insight does not delegate deployment or external-state powers. One capable actor may hold all roles within the agreed scope.

**Communication/interaction:** recipient_and_boundary: Authorised maintainers or policy holders accept changes; generators cannot enlarge their own mandate from a successful test.; content: Identify who may accept and deploy which changes, what evidence they need and when an automatic path must defer or stop.; adequacy_test: Acceptance and deployment can be traced to an authorised decision within its stated scope.; non_requirement: No separate person, meeting, registry or tool is required merely because the analytical roles are distinct.

**Assumptions:** INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-087; ACCOUNT: critical_assumption: Tool capability does not confer authority; explicit delegation can authorise bounded actions.; discriminating_question: Is the perceived new requirement actually an existing authority boundary in a new proposal channel?; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: B:ESME-064; ACCOUNT: needed: Legitimate authority, actual action capability and explicit bounds for any delegation.; failure_test: Human approval can be ceremonial or harmed by deceptive plausible patches; role separation alone does not improve correctness.; cheap_or_nontrigger: A sole authorised maintainer may generate, inspect and accept a small change without separate roles; logical distinctions do not require separate people.

Current evidence through September 2026 does not justify a timeless universal pro- or anti-automation verdict.; coupling_costs: Names or documents can conceal absent time and practical ability.; The wrong test ritual can miss operative enforcement.; Deceptive suggestions can increase review burden and survive shared oracles.; Evaluation cost, selection bias and opportunity cost.; Local success can hide mismatched identities or permissions.; A dedicated registry can add more overhead than protection.; Meta-governance can consume the capacity it is meant to protect.; Duplicated approval adds ceremony if the boundaries are already jointly satisfied.; Mandatory review can discard real narrow benefits; blind delegation can amplify failures.; Over-simplification can omit a genuinely multi-party decision.

## ESME-C098 — Non-circular, authorised revision of maintenance methods and oracles

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C098

**Canonical disposition:** ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** ANALYTICAL_EXTENSION_OF_SOURCE_MECHANISMS

**Subject/bearer:** A proposed revision of a maintenance method or oracle relative to the preceding obligation.

**Criterion:** The revision and any change of obligation have a bounded non-circular warrant; unchanged obligations remain in force and the successor does not grant itself wider authority.

**Operating mechanism:** Revise a method, threshold or oracle only for an authorised failure/benefit hypothesis or separately authorised objective change, using evidence not constituted merely by the candidate satisfying its newly chosen rule; preserve unrelated predecessor obligations.

**Trigger:** The method is failing or disproportionately costly, technology changes, or a candidate proposes to revise the expectation by which it will be judged.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Keep an adequate method or remove an unused control; independent warrant need not mean a different person or an infallible external oracle.

**Required inputs:** Evidence of the method’s limits, an authorised revision boundary and a comparison not defined solely by the proposed successor.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** The revision and any change of obligation have a bounded non-circular warrant; unchanged obligations remain in force and the successor does not grant itself wider authority.

**Consumer:** Method owners and affected consumers approve changes; the method or agent cannot authorise its own broader powers.

**Authority:** Method owners and affected consumers approve changes; the method or agent cannot authorise its own broader powers.

**Communication/interaction:** recipient_and_boundary: Method owners and affected consumers approve changes; the method or agent cannot authorise its own broader powers.; content: Revise tools, thresholds or inquiry methods through an authorised change with a stated failure/benefit hypothesis, preserving prior obligations unless separately changed.; adequacy_test: The revision has a bounded rationale and evidence, and previous obligations are not silently rewritten to make it pass.; non_requirement: No separate person, meeting, registry or tool is required merely because the analytical roles are distinct.

**Assumptions:** INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-098; ACCOUNT: critical_assumption: A warrant independent of the candidate’s success under the new rule is available; unresolved intent remains unresolved.; discriminating_question: What justifies this changed expectation other than the fact that it makes the proposed change pass?; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: B:ESME-067; ACCOUNT: needed: Evidence of the method’s limits, an authorised revision boundary and a comparison not defined solely by the proposed successor.; failure_test: This is analytical synthesis from documented method evolution, not an established universal self-revising maintenance architecture.; cheap_or_nontrigger: Keep a working method unchanged or remove an unused control; do not add reflexive governance without a demonstrated consumer.

**Costs/failure modes:** controlled_failure: A revised oracle or maintenance method provides its own only warrant, silently changing the predecessor obligation or success criterion.; known_failure_modes: INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: A:ESME-098; FINDINGS: Analytical: perfect independent truth may be unavailable; the rule requires a defensible external warrant, not impossible absolute certainty.; INPUT_PROPERTY_KEY: B:ESME-067; FINDINGS: This is analytical synthesis from documented method evolution, not an established universal self-revising maintenance architecture.; Benchmarks may leak, mix feature requests with bugs, use weak tests or exclude difficult projects. Current evidence through September 2026 does not justify a timeless universal pro- or anti-automation verdict.; coupling_costs: Premature removal can expose rare severe failures.; Meta-governance can consume the capacity it is meant to protect.; Absolute independence demands can make correction impossible.; Compatibility layers can preserve defects indefinitely.

## ESME-C102 — Cross-stage change–evidence–action coherence

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C102

**Canonical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** COMPOSITION_INDUCED

**Subject/bearer:** The cross-stage binding between intent, subject, candidate, evidence and authorised action.

**Criterion:** The evidence actually consumed and the action actually taken concern the same scoped candidate, obligations and authority; a detected mismatch blocks only the unsupported use.

**Operating mechanism:** Bind the relevant intent, baseline, candidate, impact boundary, observation/oracle and acceptance/action authority across mechanism handoffs so that evidence is consumed for the same authorised intervention.

**Trigger:** Evidence or decisions pass among stages, tools or actors, or concurrent work can change the subject before action.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** One visible issue, exact diff and direct check can establish the binding in a local change; no separate contract document or registry is required.

**Required inputs:** conditions: Constituent claims are meaningful and compatible; a document cannot create missing authority or oracle evidence.; related_property_ids: ESME-C001; ESME-C005; ESME-C022; ESME-C030; ESME-C038; interpretation: Related obligations and prerequisites, not a serial scheduling graph. Typed relations in the composition model control the relationship meaning.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** The evidence actually consumed and the action actually taken concern the same scoped candidate, obligations and authority; a detected mismatch blocks only the unsupported use.

**Consumer:** consumer: Those authorising, implementing and accepting the same intervention.; decision: Every consequential acceptance claim can be traced to the same declared change and baseline or an explicit revision.

**Authority:** actors: Those authorising, implementing and accepting the same intervention.; boundary: Understanding or recommending a change does not itself authorise altering another actor’s obligations. Use actual agreed decision rights and capability; no new role or veto is created by this record.

## ESME-C103 — Assumption-indexed evidence invalidation across mechanisms

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C103

**Canonical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Origin class:** COMPOSITION_INDUCED

**Subject/bearer:** The applicability of downstream claims to their consequential supporting assumptions.

**Criterion:** No affected claim retains unqualified operative force outside its evidence envelope; unaffected evidence remains usable.

**Operating mechanism:** Track the consequential assumptions under which evidence supports a downstream claim; when an assumption changes, narrow or invalidate only dependent claims and obtain adequate replacement evidence before consequential reuse.

**Trigger:** A relevant environment, observer, tool/model, data distribution, dependency or obligation changes while earlier evidence is still being consumed.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Reuse evidence when its relevant envelope remains valid; explicit local reasoning may replace a dependency registry or global rerun.

## ESME-C104 — Obligation continuity across reachable joint states

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C104

**Canonical disposition:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** COMPOSITION_INDUCED

**Subject/bearer:** Reachable joint states of independently considered edits, versions, mappings and responsibilities.

**Criterion:** Newly reachable consequential combinations have adequate obligation evidence or are prevented; endpoint correctness is not silently extrapolated to the join.

**Operating mechanism:** At the join of concurrent edits, mixed versions, data mappings and responsibility transfers, identify reachable combinations that matter to supported obligations and validate, isolate or prohibit those combinations.

**Trigger:** The selected composition creates materially new joint states or interactions not covered by its constituent endpoint evidence.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Proven disjointness, a genuine quiescent boundary or a simpler strategy can remove a combination; no exhaustive universal state model is required.

**Required inputs:** A credible account of reachable combinations, sequencing and external actors; abstract state models must match actual operation.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** Newly reachable consequential combinations have adequate obligation evidence or are prevented; endpoint correctness is not silently extrapolated to the join.

**Consumer:** Integrators and operators control only their own transitions; consumer/data owners adjudicate cross-boundary obligations.

**Authority:** Integrators and operators control only their own transitions; consumer/data owners adjudicate cross-boundary obligations.

**Communication/interaction:** recipient_and_boundary: Integrators and operators control only their own transitions; consumer/data owners adjudicate cross-boundary obligations.; content: Identify reachable joint states, authoritative data and permitted version/change combinations; constrain operation or add evidence for the newly created interactions.; adequacy_test: No supported transition relies solely on endpoint tests when consequential intermediate interactions remain unexamined.; non_requirement: No separate person, meeting, registry or tool is required merely because the analytical roles are distinct.

## ESME-C105 — Feedback-driven proportionality and retirement of combined controls

**Canonical global key:** EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_MAINTENANCE_AND_EVOLUTION:ESME-C105

**Canonical disposition:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

**Examination status:** EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT

**Conditionally crosswalk-worthy:** True

**Origin class:** COMPOSITION_INDUCED

**Subject/bearer:** The combined maintenance-control arrangement and its continuing consumers, protection and total burden.

**Criterion:** The selected combination has justified protection, consumers and continuation conditions; unnecessary controls can end while residual obligations remain protected.

**Operating mechanism:** Evaluate the combined inquiry, validation, coordination and support arrangement against live obligations and observed outcomes; consolidate, substitute or retire controls whose consumer or protection has ended or whose cost is dominated, without silently waiving the outcome.

**Trigger:** Several controls interact, compete for finite maintenance capacity or persist after their original obligations change.

**Non-trigger/cheap path:** Use the existing adequate local method unchanged; a simple cost/consumer judgment can suffice without a meta-process or optimisation engine.

Typed relations in the composition model control the relationship meaning.

**Outputs/evidence/constraints:** The selected combination has justified protection, consumers and continuation conditions; unnecessary controls can end while residual obligations remain protected.

**Consumer:** consumer: Maintainers and affected stakeholders deciding acceptable evidence and coordination.; decision: Each applied control has a protected failure, consumer and continuation condition; omitted alternatives have a rationale.

**Authority:** actors: Maintainers and affected stakeholders deciding acceptable evidence and coordination.; boundary: Understanding or recommending a change does not itself authorise altering another actor’s obligations. Use actual agreed decision rights and capability; no new role or veto is created by this record.

Use the cheapest adequate channel in NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH; mere publication is not successful communication.
